## 16cn. Session-100 Layer (the diff-clustering decode promoted + the 1280 xl-boundary spot-check)

**The 100-c rotation — the twice-suggested tool promotion.** Every
genus hunt since s95 decoded its deltas with one-off bucket-diff
probes; `bun run sweep -- --clusters` now decodes each page's diff
into BUCKETS — clustered regions of differing pixels (a coarse-grid
union-find, cell = `--cluster-gap` px [default 16]; clusters closer
than ~gap px merge), each reported with its bbox, pixel count, share
of the page's diff, and share of the frame (top 5 per page, biggest
genus first). The decode answers WHERE a diff lives (the rail band vs
the content band) before any DOM probe is written.

**The seam contract.** `clusterDiff(a, b, tol, w, h, gap)` sits beside
`diffPixels` with the IDENTICAL tolerance semantics; the size-mismatch
throw parity; the non-positive-gap fail-fast (the B-97a2 doctrine).
The browser-side diffPair evaluate carries the inline twin (the
no-bundling doctrine — the pure seam is what the unit pins exercise in
node). The gate semantics are UNCHANGED (the drift-gate output
byte-identical with or without --clusters).

**The maiden 1280×800 spot-check (the s99 suggested next #3).** The
s99 prediction CONFIRMED: accounts 0.00% (was 2.43% at 1024) — the
rail-squeeze genus is DEAD at 1280; every page within the DESKTOP
standing baselines (dashboard 0.36 · the rest 0.00–0.01 · settings
4.67 · login 0.34) — the 1280 → desktop class assignment holds, NO
fifth class, gate:full unchanged. The cluster fingerprints at 1280
match the 1440 desktop genera (the dashboard chart-artifact bucket ·
the settings picklist band · the login logo genus).

**The tool validated against the KNOWN decode.** The landscape 1024
re-run with --clusters reproduces F-99c1 exactly — one dominant
accounts bucket [x 356..1023, y 303..767] at 95.4% of the diff (the
content/rail divergence band), settings' picklist genus at 100% of
diff in one content band, the single-pixel sub-pixel-noise buckets
isolated as 1px slivers instead of polluting the genus reads.

**The audit finds (100-a, both REAL docs-only, both FIXED).**
F-100a1: PAD:779 + SKILL §16cm:8910 said "3 new" its where the true
count is 2 (the same rows' own "+2 its = 1872" arithmetic) — the
loose "2 new pins … + the explained line" phrasing seeded the error.

edited "3 new pins" → "2 new pins" by the s99 delta — internally
contradictory with its own "3 failed | 9 passed → 12/12"; reverted.
The lesson: a history block is a RECORD, not a living count site —
never edit a prior session's numbers to match a later arithmetic.

## 16co. Session-101 Layer (the edge-wrap fix + the drift probe promoted + the 375 drift-gate maiden)

**The context.** Session 101 opened at `main` @ `ba18bb3` (the s100
ship + the operator's docs-only `session_202.md`). Baseline gate
GREEN at exactly the s100 ship state (lint 0/0 · tsc 0 · 1880/1880);
census MATCH; the environment intact from s100 (no rebuild needed).
Both audits ran as subagents in parallel.

**The 101-a find — F-101a1, the edge-wrap defect.** The audit's
pre-fix live proof: the s100 clusterDiff's union-find neighbor lookup
`nk = (cy+dy)*cw + (cx+dx)` has no column-bounds guard, so a cell at
grid column 0 probing dx=-1 computes a key that ALIASES to the LAST
column of the adjacent row — two 16×16 diff blocks at x 0..15 and x
144..159 (129px apart, 9 grid columns apart at gap 16) merged into
ONE bucket [x 0..159]. The decode's bbox/share/top-5 outputs were
corrupted for any diff touching opposite horizontal edges of the
frame within a row band. The fix, RED-first pinned then applied in
BOTH copies (the node seam + the browser inline twin — the
no-bundling doctrine): `if (cx + dx < 0 || cx + dx >= cw) continue;`.
Vertical out-of-range keys can never collide with a real marked cell
(the key space is bounded top and bottom by the grid), so the
horizontal guard is the complete fix. No prior --clusters output
changes (no real page diff touches both frame edges in one band) —
the fix is correctness-for-defects-yet-unseen.

**The rotation (101-c) — the drift probe promoted.** Every session
since s92 re-derived the same three-step reference-stability probe
by hand (the bundle md5 + the census + the defect check). The
promotion: `scripts/drift-probe.ts` + `bun run probe:ref` — login →
the POST-LOGIN script inventory (script srcs + modulepreload hrefs)
→ fetch each → the largest JS by bytes is the app shell → the
node:crypto md5 → the demo-data census (contacts/leads/accounts data
rows; the single-cell colspan empty-state row is excluded by the
>1-cells predicate) → the desktop nav census (256px/8 visible links
via getClientRects) → the TRUE-390 census (nav w=0 · 0 visible links
· 0 menu buttons — the reference's own defect, 22nd consecutive) →
the verdict report; exit 1 on any CHANGED (gateable); `--json` for
machine-readable. The PURE seams (pickAppBundle · stabilityVerdict ·
desktopNavVerdict · mobileNavVerdict · demoDataVerdict · STANDING)
are pinned in tests/drift-probe-tool.test.ts — the same node-side
contract the live run reports against. NOT part of gate/gate:full:
the reference is a network dependency and the offline gate must stay
offline-runnable. A NAV-FIXED verdict (the reference grows phone
navigation) is the parity re-evaluation trigger, not a failure.

**The maiden runs.** The probe's first run reproduced every standing
layer exactly: bundle `index-DZ-xbrIm.js` · md5
`a70a637fcf1d4291da8e0d965676dc11` · 1,631,071 bytes — STABLE (the
72nd consecutive); census #97 demo zero · desktop 256px/8 STANDING ·
the mobile-nav defect STANDS at TRUE 390 (22nd consecutive). The
MAIDEN 375×812 drift-gate spot-check (`bun run sweep -- --width 375
--height 812 --fail-on-drift`): CLEAN — floor 0.55–0.59 · settings
7.52 · login 0.82, every page within the phone baselines walked at
390; the s96 plain-run prediction is now GATE-proven at the second
walked width. All four standing drift gates re-verified CLEAN
post-fix (desktop 1440 standing table reproduced · phone 390 ·
tablet 768 · landscape 1024 with accounts 2.43 the known genus); the
drawer battery FULLY GREEN live at TRUE 390.

**The docs finds fixed.** F-101a2: the F-100a1 "3→2" fix had
over-reached into the PAD s99 row's Files column — under the table's
own s93–s98 semantics (Files = files gaining NEW its: 1/1/1/3/2/3)
the true value is 1; fixed, and the s100 row's B-101a-1 drift (2→1)
aligned with the same semantics. N-101a-2: the AGENTS:3938 missing
space. N-101a-1 (the "non-positive gap" phrasing vs the `gap < 1`
guard — the error message itself is accurate) recorded in the plan,
no edit.

**The pins.** RED-first: 2 new sweep-tool its (the edge-wrap
geometry pin — blocks at OPPOSITE horizontal edges of one row band
stay TWO buckets with exact bboxes; the browser-twin guard wiring
pin — the guard text present TWICE, the no-bundling doctrine) + the
NEW tests/drift-probe-tool.test.ts (10 its: the pick/stability/nav/
demoData/STANDING seam family + the wiring pin incl. the
offline-gate negative) + the CLAUDE-count lockstep re-anchor (1880 →

## 16cv. Session-108 Layer (the fourth-surface seeded mobile walk + the two live finds + the four stale-docs fixes)

The rotation: the s107-close suggested next #1 — the fourth-surface
seeded walk (the calendar, the remaining un-walked phone surface with
the app's tightest grid geometry: 7 tracks in ~326px ≈ 43px cells).
**The ANALYZE probes surfaced TWO LIVE FINDS before any guard was
written** — the exact genus the walk family exists to catch, both
invisible to every standing gate:

- **M-108c1 — the chips render at CONTENT width.** The event chips
  (and "+N more") are INLINE spans; `truncate` (overflow:hidden +
  ellipsis + nowrap) never constrains an inline box. LIVE-measured on
  the seeded build: 93-177px chips inside 43px cells at 390 — two
  pushing `main.scrollWidth` to **496** (a horizontally-swipeable
  main at /calendar) — and 134-203px chips inside 97px cells at 1440
  (7+ chips visually overlapping the neighboring cells' content).
  Why invisible: the drift sweeps run the ZERO-DATA state (no events
  → no chips) and the ten-route overflow sweep asserts `docScrollW`
  only (the document never overflows — main is the scroller). The
  reference's own construction (the s76 L-76c9 bundle decode: the
  plain `space-y-0.5` stack + the `cursor-pointer truncate rounded
  px-1 py-0.5 text-xs` span) carries the same latent defect —
  un-live-verifiable at its zero-data state; ours the documented
  superset (the mobile-nav doctrine): `block` on both spans makes the
  truncation engage at every width.
- **M-108c2 — the 42-cell grid vs the reference's complete-weeks
  grid.** The s76 M-76c8 decode gloss ("ALWAYS the UNTRIMMED 42
  cells — 6 constant rows") is a MISDECODE. LIVE-probed on the
  reference at five months: **Oct 2026 = 35 [Sep 27..Oct 31] · Nov
  2026 = 35 [Nov 1..Dec 5] · Jan 2027 = 42 [Dec 27..Feb 6, the
  6-week month] · Apr 2027 = 35 [Mar 28..May 1] · Jul 2026 = 35
  [Jun 28..Aug 1]** — the COMPLETE-WEEKS model
  (eachDayOfInterval(Sunday-before-the-1st,
  Saturday-on-or-after-the-last): 28/35/42 as the month demands),
  exactly the s51-era "trimmed render" the s51 test comments describe
  ("Nov 2026: lead 0 + 30 days → 5 weeks → Dec 5"). Why invisible:
  the extra 6th row sits BELOW the 900px sweep fold on both apps
  (VLM-verified off the sweep screenshots: rows [27..3][4..10]
  [11..17][18..24-cut] — the 0.01% desktop diff is fold-below), and
  no e2e pinned a cell count. The fix: `calendarGrid`'s leading
  branch ceils to whole weeks (`Math.ceil((offset + daysInMonth) /
  7) * 7`) — the page call byte-identical (the s76 GUARD source pin
  keeps passing), `calendarFetchBounds` follows exactly (the N-51a
  contract now with EQUALITY: Oct → Oct 31 · Nov → Dec 5 · Jan 2027
  → Feb 6).

The ship: the NEW tests/calendar-grid.test.ts (3 its — the
live-verified members table + the structural invariants over every
month of 2026-2027 + the fetch-bounds alignment) + the calendar-cells
chip pin (+1 it) + the NEW tests/e2e/calendar-mobile.spec.ts (the
fourth-surface walk: docScrollW AND **mainScrollW** ≤ 390 — the NEW
inner-overflow assertion, the M-108c1 catcher — + the KPI ladder +
the DOW/month grids + the chip containment + the complete-weeks cell
count computed in-test + the REAL month-nav round-trip) + the four
audit finds fixed + pinned (F-108a1 the missing s107 project_state
layer inserted + the layer-axis lockstep assertions; F-108a2 the
page-layout counts 1530/182; F-108a3 the reports-data row 279 +
the reportsKpis clause; F-108a4 the DEPLOYMENT 27 handlers + the
deleted smoke-test.sh reference) + the count ladder (E2E 135 → 136 ·
UNIT 1927 → 1932 · SUITES 107 → 108 · badge 2062 → 2068 · the HEAD
template "in 6 spec" · the S16-P7 e2e `≥ 28` re-anchor) + the probe
ordinals at the 79th/29th. RED-first 27 failed | 55 passed (82),

## 16cz. Session-112 Layer (the dialog-family LIVE re-walk + the four finds)

The s111-close's third candidate taken: the DIALOG family — the
largest interactive surface group never walked live at BOTH widths
(the s92 walk was 390-ONLY and 20 sessions of changes ago; the
dialogs' mount-focus behavior — the s111 D1 genus — never measured).
A throwaway walk script (repo-internal, deleted before ship) drove
BOTH apps through the CREATE-dialog ladder — New Lead · New Account ·
New Contact · Import Contacts · New Event · Log Call · Save Custom
Report View — at 1440×900 + 390×844, our side on the standalone build
+ a throwaway walk db, extracting per dialog the content-box geometry,
the overlay, the header, the close X, the first field group, the
footer, the MOUNT-FOCUS state, the child census, and the document
scroll widths.

Three walk-script iterations (the s111 three-iteration precedent):
(1) `page.evaluate(STRING)` evaluates the string to a FUNCTION OBJECT
— non-serializable, returns undefined; (2) the pre-hydration click
swallow (the retry then blind-clicked behind the opened overlay — 30s
actionability hangs; fixed with the xhr-aware settle + short click
timeouts + the late-open round loop); (3) **the D-112a4 find** — our
PERMANENTLY-MOUNTED mobile-nav drawer carries `role="dialog"` with no
data-state: CSS `[role=dialog]` queries match IT first and never
resolve (getByRole is safe — Playwright excludes hidden elements).
The rule is documented at the source.

**THE RESULT — the dialog family is in COMPLETE LIVE PARITY**: every
geometry axis PARITY on all 7 dialogs × 2 viewports (the widths 512/
672/448 at 1440, full-bleed 390 at phone; the max-h-[90vh] scroll
caps; the pads/radius/gaps; the header alignment ladders; the titles
+ description arms; the close X geometry; the label→control gaps; the
footers + submits; the census structure) AND the mount-focus behavior
IDENTICAL on all seven (the first input on lead/account/event/
savereport · the camera button on contact · the type select trigger
on activity · the Cancel on import). The noise families adjudicated
away: the oklab/rgba overlay serialization, the transparent-shadow
prepends (the focused-input ring computed-equal — the identical
visible 1px #0a0a0a ring + shadow-sm), the labelMb 8px-vs-0 v3/v4
space-y mechanism flip, and the border-input/border-line + trigger
w-full orderings (the s86 computed-equal notes).

The four finds: **D-112a1** the close-X byte-parity re-pin (the
reference's CURRENT stock close carries `ring-offset-background` +
the two inert `data-[state=open]` arms and NOT `disabled:opacity-70`;
our s15-era string predates its shadcn bump — the two inert arms
gained, the inert arm retired, `ring-offset-background` the
COMPUTED-CONTRACT EXCEPTION deliberately absent: the ref's
focused-close offset computes hsl(0 0% 100%) = #fff = our v4 default;
the class would set #f9fafb — the s11 computed-gap rule) · **D-112a2**
the camera aria-label superset pin (the reference's icon-only button
UNNAMED — its own a11y defect; ours the mobile-nav genus) ·
**D-112a3** the six mount-focus e2e freezes (added expects inside
EXISTING test() blocks — the e2e census stays 137) · **D-112a4** the
role=dialog shadowing rule (documented at the mobile-nav source).

files) · SUITES 109 UNCHANGED · E2E 137 UNCHANGED · badge 2079 → 2081
· the page-layout census rows 1530 → 1548 + 182 → 183 checks (the

## 16da. Session-113 Layer (the menu/popover-family LIVE re-walk + the three finds)

The rotation was scout-adjudicated before it was walked: a two-app
probe of the s112-close's three candidates found the topbar search
renders NO dropdown on EITHER app (zero popper surfaces on click and on
fill — the s32 dead-decode confirmed live, our functional superset
unchanged) and the reports chart tooltips are recharts defaults on both
sides (4 wrappers, invisible pre-hover — the s10 doctrine). The
menu/popover family was the only walkable candidate — and the right
one: the s13-era MENU_CONTENT/MENU_ITEM pins were class-string
extractions from 2026-09-30, thirteen sessions and one reference
shadcn bump ago; the family's mount-focus + keyboard behavior (the
s111 D1 genus) had never been measured; the Filters popover's
field-label TAGS had never been census'd.

**The walk:** two throwaway scripts (deleted before ship) drove BOTH
apps through the account-menu + leads-Filters + leads-row-menu ladder
at 1440×900 + 390×844 — our side on the standing build + a scratch walk
db — extracting per surface: the trigger census (geometry + full class
string), the open panel geometry (x/y/w/h/data-side/data-align), the
computed styles, the popper transform, the item census
(tags/texts/hrefs/classes/computed), the MOUNT-FOCUS state, the
ArrowDown highlight, and the Escape close.

**The result:** the family is in COMPLETE LIVE PARITY on every computed
axis. The account menu's geometry is byte-identical at 1440 (x=1280
y=56 w=128 h=74, bottom/end, the popper transform `matrix(1,0,0,1,
1280,56)` on BOTH apps); the content computes equal on every axis
(white / 1px #e5e5e5 / 6px radius / shadow-md's two visible layers /
4px pad / #0a0a0a / 128px min-w); the item census is identical
([A/Profile href="/Profile", DIV/Logout] · 118×32 · 6px 8px · 4px ·
14px/400); the mount-focus is identical (the content DIV holds focus on
click-open); the keyboard model is identical (ArrowDown lands the first
item with data-highlighted + the #f5f5f5 wash). The Filters popover's
geometry is byte-identical at BOTH widths (320×398 @304,483 at 1440;
the 390 collision FLIP-UP to data-side=top matched) with the census
identical (2 selects · 2 inputs · the All Status/All Sources/Clear/
Save View buttons). The row menus are ours-only walkable — the
reference's persistent zero-data state renders no rows (the documented
genus; the s73 bundle decode is the reference contract) — ours measured
188×106 bottom/end with the Edit/Convert-to-Opportunity/DEAD/Delete
census and the content mount-focus, all consistent with the stock Menu
family.

**The finds:**

- **D-113a1 (REAL, structural/a11y)**: the reference's Filters popover
  renders FOUR bare `<label>` elements (`text-sm font-medium mb-2
  block`, unassociated); ours shipped the same four as `<span>`s. Real
  label semantics (the a11y tree + the form-labeling family) — the
  strict-mirror fix at the four leads-page sites; the bare-label
  construction byte-matches the reference (no htmlFor on either side).
- **D-113a2 (inert class-string — the s112 D-112a1 genus)**: our
  s13-era MENU_CONTENT carried `outline-none`; the reference's CURRENT
  stock content does not (the s13 dump predates its shadcn bump).
  Retired for byte parity — verified INERT live on both open paths (the
  click-open's programmatic focus on the content is not :focus-visible;
  the keyboard-open focuses the first ITEM — the content never holds
  focus-visible). The token-map arms (border-line/bg-surface/
  text-foreground for border/bg-popover/text-popover-foreground) STAY —
  the documented s73 computed-equal mechanism, live-verified
  (white/#e5e5e5/#0a0a0a on both apps; the s86 border-input precedent).
  The ITEM's own outline-none stays (the reference's stock menuitem
  DOES carry it). The Popover-family DropdownContent base keeps its own
  outline-none + min-w-[10rem] + overflow-hidden + z-[60] (the
  reference's POPOUR stock carries outline-none; the rest are the inert
  extras of our superset-family base — documented, not re-pinned).
- **D-113a3 (behavioral, UNPINNED)**: the mount-focus + keyboard model
  frozen as e2e expects INSIDE existing test() blocks (the census stays
  137) — the account menu's content focus, the ArrowDown first-item
  landing, the row menu's content focus. The s111 D1 lesson: behavioral
  parity no class-string pin can see.

**The noise families adjudicated away:** the ring-arms' string position
(the s86 ordering — the account trigger's arms ride after the svg
group on ours, before the disabled arms on the reference, computed
equal); the transparent-shadow prepends (the s112 v4 serialization
noise — the visible shadow-md layers identical); the token-map
mechanism (s73/s86); the topbar @390 arrangement (the s93 standing
genus — the walk re-confirmed both censuses exactly: the reference's
lone account chip at x=16, ours [hamburger LEFT] + [chip RIGHT], the
documented mobile-nav superset arrangement).

labels it, both in page-layout.test.ts) · SUITES 109 UNCHANGED · E2E
137 UNCHANGED (the focus expects land inside existing blocks) · badge

## 16db. Session-114 Layer (the profile + settings Data-tab family live walk + the three finds)

The s113 close offered three candidates and the scout killed one
before the walk: the sidebar/topbar chrome re-walk was ELIMINATED on
principle — the chrome rides every pixel sweep at all four viewports
every session, so the drift gates ARE its standing live walk. What
remained was two interlocked personal-workspace surfaces never
live-walked: the /Profile page (the photo interaction family) and the
settings Data tab (the export/import family). One walk covered both,
plus a download axis the scout settled in a single pass: the three CSV
templates download md5-IDENTICAL on both apps, and the reference's
zero-data exports are EMPTY files — the s26 live verification
re-confirmed, our seeded dumps the s26-P4 bundle-decoded contract.

The walk itself (four throwaway scripts, both apps, 1440×900 +
390×844, the standalone build on a scratch walk db) found a surface in
near-complete live parity: the /Profile geometry byte-identical at both
widths (the grid 896×594 at 1440 and 358×1236 at 390; the personal
card 589×594; the four side cards 283×224/98/98/98; the avatar 96/80
with the blue-100 inner and the stroke-2 user glyph; the camera button
159×36 at 537,312; the nameGap 12), the computed styles equal on every
axis, and the interaction battery identical: the camera click opens
the file chooser on both apps, the upload round-trip posts and swaps
the avatar preview with the same toast, and the save round-trip is
UNCONDITIONAL on both (the button never gates on dirty — the s62
decode live-confirmed) with the same reload behavior. The Data tab's
cards compute byte-equal (the Import and Export cards 1120×160 both,
the seven buttons' texts identical, the tablist-to-panel gap 24 on
both — the panel's mt-2 collapsing against the wrapper's space-y-6).

The finds were three. The visual one was four pixels with a one-class
cause: the Danger Zone warning line. The reference renders it at
text-sm text-red-600 — 14px/20px, a 20px line inside a 240px card —
while ours rendered the bare text-red-600 at 16px/24px, the +4px
propagating the whole inner ladder of the card (244px, every child
shifted down four). The s26 live DOM probe had under-read the line
(the s112 D-112a1 genus — our pin older than the reference's current
construction), and the drift sweeps never see the Data tab because
they sweep the DEFAULT settings tab. The fix was one class: text-sm
joined SETTINGS_DANGER.desc for byte parity. The construction find was
the form-labeling family again (the s113 D-113a1 genus): the reference
wires its profile labels and inputs with the BARE email/role id pairs
(the display_name pair already matched ours byte-for-byte), while ours
shipped profile-email/profile-role — an early defensive uniqueness
with no collision to defend against (the only other id="email" in src/
is the login card, a different route). The four attribute swaps
retired it. The third find was a freeze, not a fix: the interaction
battery — the camera/file-input wiring, the upload round-trip, the
unconditional save — measured identical and pinned (two unit its in
profile-family-parity + the upload and save round-trip expects inside
the existing /Profile e2e test, the census staying 137).

The noise families adjudicated away: the shadow transparent-prepends
(the s112 v4 serialization noise — the visible layers identical); the
side chips (ours span + inline style, the reference div + classes —
computed-equal on every axis, the s73 mechanism); the class orderings
(s86); the token-maps (s73/s86); the avatar border-radius 9999px vs
33554432px (the v3/v4 rounded-full computed-value genus — both fully
round, visually identical); the Danger confirm-label ASSOCIATION (ours
wires htmlFor="reset-confirm" + id, the reference ships a bare
unassociated label — ours the a11y superset, the s112 D-112a2 genus,
KEPT); the nameGap 12 (the s91 controlMt compensator validated live
again); and the reference's toast auto-dismiss timing (its toasts
dismiss inside any fixed scan window — the s30/s62 bundle decodes
remain the vocabulary contract).

**The count ladder:** UNIT 1946 → 1948 (+2 — the profile email/role id
pin + the camera/file-input wiring pin, both in
profile-family-parity.test.ts) · SUITES 109 UNCHANGED · E2E 137
UNCHANGED (the upload + save expects land inside the existing /Profile
test) · badge 2083 → 2085 · the page-layout census row 1559 → 1566
(the D-114a1 walk-note comment growth; the checks count UNCHANGED at

**F-121c1 — THE REAL DEFECT (the walk's find, FIXED as D-121a0):** the
standalone server wrote uploads to `<repo>/.next/standalone/uploads` —
the EPHEMERAL build output, wiped by every `bun run build` (every
stored photo_url 404s after a rebuild; dev and standalone diverged).
Root cause (instrumented live on the built chunk): the standalone
bundle rewrites `import.meta.url` into the VIRTUAL path
`<repo>/.next/standalone/src/lib/uploads.ts`; uploads.ts's
module-URL-first anchor computed `dirname/../.. = .next/standalone` and
VALIDATED it as a repo root — the output-file trace puts
`prisma/schema.prisma` INSIDE `.next/standalone`. db-path's session-34
order already guards this exact trap (the standalone-cwd anchor FIRST,
"the standalone bundle rewrites import.meta.url into a virtual path
that must be ignored"). The fix mirrors it: the cwd anchor FIRST + the
pure `repoRootFor(moduleUrl, cwd)` seam, pinned by the NEW
tests/uploads-path.test.ts (the buildStandaloneRepo fixture idiom —
the trap pin RED on the module-first order, stash-proven by reverting
the order alone) and verified live by the PASS 2 re-run (65/65, the
uploads at `<repo>/uploads`, the standalone location EMPTY).

**The remediation:** D-121a0 [src/lib/uploads.ts + the NEW
uploads-path suite (4 its)] + D-121a1 the drawer-battery REPO
derivation (the 121-b audit's N-121b1 — the hardcoded
`/home/z/my-project/neo-crm` went stale on relocation; now derived
from the script's own location, verified live: the battery boots its
own server, exit 0) + D-121a2 the both-avatar-carriers pin (the table
row AND the mobile card `photoUrl` conditionals — the lg:hidden face
pinned on NO axis before) + D-121a3 the profile 5MB-hint +
Uploading-ternary pins + D-121a4 the docs realignment + session_260 +
the captures 212-215.

gate lint 0/0 · tsc 0 · 110/1964 · build · 137/137; the audits 121-a
[9/9 Verified] + 121-b [9/9 PASS, ~79th standing]; probe:ref the 92nd
STABLE; the four drift gates CLEAN; census MATCH; src/ 2 files changed
(uploads.ts + drawer-battery-390.ts — the fix session).

**The walk-harness lessons (record-only):** L-121c2 the next-server
process rename (the standalone server renames itself `next-server
(v<next-version>)` — `pgrep -f server.js` never matches it; stale servers
survive naive kills and EADDRINUSE the next boot — kill by the REAL

server.js &` backgrounds the CHAIN — `$!` is the subshell, killing it

walk family's exact purpose — every one invisible to the standing
gates):

- **F-123a1 (pre-walk, tooling)**: the platform shell exports a STALE
  absolute `DATABASE_URL` (the relocated-workspace path); the unguarded
  `bun run dev` spawns in sweep.ts + drawer-battery-390.ts inherited it

  guard into the tools themselves (the Session-93 F-A `delete` rule,
  extended from the zero-data spawn to the boot sites).
- **F-123a2 (the BOM)**: our header CSV's download face shipped
  `\uFEFF` (57 bytes at zero rows); the reference's own client-side
  artifact is BOM-less (54 bytes, otherwise byte-identical). The s25
  "BOM keeps Excel happy" convention was OUR route's choice, never a
  decoded reference fact. The route now serves the artifact verbatim on
  both faces; the e2e BOM assertion updated in place.
- **F-123a3 (the per-table PDF geometry family)**: the reference's
  text PDFs decode to MM-ANCHORED constants — constructor `unit: "mm"`,
  margin 14mm, the title **Helvetica-NORMAL** 16pt at (14, 15), the
  Generated line normal-10 at (14, 22), the column headers **bold 9pt**
  at x = 14 + i×60mm y = 32mm (the FLAT 60mm spacing, not span-derived),
  the line width = jsPDF's unit-mm DEFAULT flush (0.567pt — never
  setLineWidth; an explicit call emits a second, differently-rounded
  operator), and **NO header rule at zero rows** (the rule gated on
  rows existing; its populated geometry stays ours — the unknowable
  face). VERIFIED: the mm-unit candidate's content stream is
  BYTE-EQUAL to the reference's zero-row artifact modulo the same-day
  date; the post-fix artifacts are SIZE-EXACT (3563/3559 bytes).
- **F-123a4 (the dialog Save button's BLACK pair)**: the reference's
  "Save Report" computes bg rgb(23,23,23) + #fafafa — the DIALOG_SUBMIT
  black pair its entity dialogs ride (its "Create Contact" verified

- **F-123a5 (the bar Export CSV's literal pair)**: the reference's
  filter-bar Export CSV carries literal `bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700
  text-white` (bg byte-equal; the text computes #ffffff vs our token
  #fafafa). The reference's own mixed usage: its toolbars ride the
  s119 token pair, this bar rides literals — mirrored per-surface.
- **F-123a6 (the v4 space-y trailing-margin genus)**: Tailwind v4's
  `space-y` emits margin-BOTTOM (all-but-last) where the reference's
  v3-style emits margin-TOP. Visually identical (collapse), but our
  trailing mb=24 extended main's scrollHeight to **1545 vs the
  reference's 1521** at zero-data (the PDF capture height + the scroll
  extent — invisible to the viewport-based drift gates). The reports
  Tabs wrapper's `space-y-6` retired; the panels' `mt-2` → `mt-6` (the
  reference's computed mt=24 geometry, no trailing margin). The
  dialog-geometry space-y census re-anchored 108 → 107 (the s96/s98

**THE FIND — F-125c1 THE SEGMENTED ACTIVE-PILL BG** (the walk's one
real parity defect, the genus every prior gate was blind to): the
active segmented tab computed `rgb(249,250,251)` on ours vs
`rgb(255,255,255)` on the reference — live-scoped FOUR-WAY (ours +
reference × /activities + /settings): the tracks (`rgb(245,245,245)`
h-36 p-1), the inactive tabs (transparent + `#737373`), and the
geometry all byte-equal; ONLY the active pill diverged. **The root
cause**: the reference's own construction rides
`data-[state=active]:bg-background`, which resolves to ITS stock
shadcn zinc background `#ffffff`; OUR theme deliberately redefined
`--color-background` to `#f9fafb` (the app's page bg, the visual
spec) — the token ride silently drifted 6 rgb units. **The
F-119c2 inverted-token-mapping genus** (the reference's `--primary`
dark vs our blue — the token SEMANTICS diverge while the literal
computed surfaces must match): the fix is the LITERAL
`data-[state=active]:bg-white` (src/lib/page-layout.ts
TABS_SEGMENTED.trigger), live-verified byte-equal post-fix on BOTH
segmented surfaces. **The blindness analysis**: the pixel sweeps'
0.5% margin swallows a 6-rgb-unit pill on a 36px track; the s106/
s115 settings walks measured the strip geometry + the trigger texts
but never censused the active pill's computed bg; the s109 mobile
walk was ours-only. **The lesson**: on token-riding surfaces, census
the COMPUTED value of every state arm (active/inactive/hover),
two-app — the token mapping genus makes source-parity insufficient.

**L-125c2 (adjudicated noise)**: the More Filters (1) expander —
ours EXPANDS (revealing the Task/Note group, the functional superset
over the reference's documented dead stub; the fix-over-defect
doctrine, the same genus as the dead Owner/Status selects ours also
makes functional). Recorded, never reverted.

**The remediation**: D-125a0 the active-pill fix (TABS_SEGMENTED) ·
D-125a1 the pin (+1 it, page-layout, RED-first — the s12 trigger
pin's token ride re-anchored to the literal + the retired-form
negative) · D-125a2..a6 THE WALK PINS (+8 its,
activities-calendar-parity: the tab-label quartet + the empty-state
quartet + the KPI label sextet [the D-117a2/D-118a1 genus] + the
FILTERED-basis doctrine + the activities API order [dueAt asc,
createdAt desc] + the quick-log preset + the toggle arms + the
by-type vocabulary) · D-125a7 the docs + the captures 228-231 + the
VLM battery 4/4 × 4 CLEAN (231's badge-overlap flag ADJUDICATED a
VLM misread — the geometry probe: the 8px gaps everywhere,
docScrollW/mainScrollW 390/390).

**The audits**: 125-a 9/9 shipped claims Verified (zero code
findings; the N-125a1/a2 record nanos) + the full activities
pre-walk inventory; 125-b 8/9 PASS — **F-125b1** the platform
DATABASE_URL poisoning the stand-up db:push/db:seed onto the
sandbox-root mirror (the absolute override honored as "intentional"
by the db-path seam design; remediated by the env -u reseed; the
walk-harness lesson: on a fresh stand-up ALWAYS reseed via `env -u
DATABASE_URL bun run db:push && env -u DATABASE_URL bun run
db:seed`, then census via `env -u DATABASE_URL bun run db:census` —
the resolved path is the evidence, a count without its path is not).
probe:ref the 97th STABLE · the four drift gates CLEAN · scandihaven
d4789c3 on-pattern.

source) → the fix → GREEN → **the 9 stash-proofs** (the src fix

## 16dn. Session-126 Layer (the login-family second-cycle re-check — the first re-walk of the second rotation cycle)

**The rotation**: the session_269 close's named direction — the second
rotation cycle begins (re-checks of the earlier walks: login s111,
dialogs s112, menus s113 the stalest), login chosen (15 sessions back;
the source FROZEN since s111 but the LENSES sharpened since: the

the live timings).

**The walk**: both apps at 1440×900 + 390×844 through the five-view
ladder (signin → reset → sent → signup → verify) + the wrong-password
callout (1 attempt) + the mismatch guard + the wrong-code rung (1
attempt) + the authed-visitor card service (the S23-P2 first two-app
live confirmation) + PASS 2 ours-only (the info-callout auto-dismiss
~3.4s + the db round-trip: 4 seeded + 3 walk signups = 7 users) —
330 axes, 24 raw diffs → **THREE REAL PARITY DEFECTS**:

1. **F-126c1 THE APOSTROPHE GENUS**: ours ships typographic `&rsquo;`
   where the reference ships straight ASCII `'` — the reset
   description "we'll", the sent view "We've", the verify email line
   "We've", the resend line "Didn't" (4 source sites, never text-pinned
   before — the e2e assertions had ridden OUR curly forms). The genus
   lesson: TEXT CONTENT is a parity axis the computed-style censuses
   never covered — census the DOM text verbatim, not just the styles.
2. **F-126c2 THE AUTH-INPUT TEXT COLOR**: ours `text-slate-900`
   `rgb(15,23,42)` vs the reference's `rgb(9,9,11)` on all 5 censused
   inputs (signin/reset/signup). The fix retires the class — the typed
   ink inherits `--color-foreground` `#0a0a0a` (within the standing
   fuzzy-2 of the reference's zinc-950-ish ink); the TITLES keep
   slate-900 (the reference's own titles ARE slate-900 — the same page
   carries BOTH inks in the reference's own construction).
3. **F-126c3 THE CODE-INPUT BG — the F-125c1 genus on the login
   surface**: the verify code inputs ride `bg-background` — our token
   resolves to the page-bg `#f9fafb` vs the reference's stock white.
   The login surface is PRE-AUTH: invisible to probe:ref's post-login
   bundle AND to the drift gates' authenticated screenshots — only a
   walk sees it. Fixed with the LITERAL `bg-white` (the D-125a0
   doctrine).

**The adjudicated noise**: L-126c4 the code inputs' aria-label
superset (the L-124c1 a11y doctrine genus — ours "Digit N" over the
reference's unnamed inputs, fixed in ours, never reverted) · L-126c5
the body-bg invisible axis (ours `#f9fafb` vs the reference's white —
the F-119c2 token semantics under a full-cover gradient wrapper, never
visible) · L-126c6 the 1-unit ring/ink residue (ours `#0a0a0a` vs the
reference's `#09090b` — within the standing fuzzy-2; the reference's
own explicit zinc-950 construction).

**The byte-equal contracts**: the s111 byte-matches ALL HOLD (the card
heights 470/422 · 488/412 at both widths) + the card chrome + the
accent/logo/Google/divider/labels/inputs/submits/footer censuses + the
reset/sent/signup/verify full censuses + the error/sent callouts +
the containment + the authed-visitor service.

**The remediation**: D-126a0 the apostrophe fix (4 sites + the 3 e2e
assertions updated in place — the s123 BOM precedent) · D-126a1 the
auth-input text-color fix (the 3 INPUT records) · D-126a2 the
code-input bg fix · D-126a3 the pins (+3 its, login-family-parity,
RED-first; the s79 code-arm pin re-anchored in place) · D-126a4 the
docs + the captures 232-235 + the VLM battery 4/4 × 4 CLEAN (233
reading the STRAIGHT apostrophes).

**The walk-harness lessons (all record-only)**:
- **L-126c7 the pydantic reserved-TLD rejection**: the reference's
  signup rejects `.test` emails ("special-use or reserved name") —
  walk emails must use plausible domains or the signup-driven views
  never load.
- **L-126c8 the oklab inverse**: the cube goes AFTER the linear
  transform (the inverse yields cube-rooted LMS coords; cubing L
  first produced phantom 5-unit color diffs that masqueraded as
  defects — the input-bg "divergence" was a converter bug).
- **L-126c9 the via-stop 50% serialization**: v4 serializes the
  explicit `50%` on via-stops, v3 omits it — normalize in gradient
  compares (the via stop is semantically always 50%).
- **L-126c10 the zero-extent shadow layers**: drop any
  `0px 0px 0px 0px` layer regardless of color (the reference's own v3
  serialization carries a dead white one on its code-input ring).

- **L-128c3 the Playwright hasText regex whitespace genus**: the regex
  form (`{ hasText: /^Filters/ }`) matches the RAW textContent —

  Next.js 16 standalone server renames its process title to
  `next-server (v<next-version>)` after boot — kill-by-`$!`-PID +
  `pkill -f 'standalone/server.js'` do not reach it; sweep by the
  `next-server` comm (the title's version literal is era-dependent —
  s170 re-derived from the live 16.4.2 title, the advice version-free).

reference's platform avoids the state entirely (its density select carries the

inherits the foreground ink. The s81 M-81c3 bundle re-derivation already
established the reference's verbatim construction carries NO placeholder arm —

dead-layer shadow genus · the s95 containment cascade @390 (both toolbars stack
identically — the rows exactly 220px both apps, ours 326 contained vs the ref's
440 poke) · the climb-guard harness artifact (the fixed probe: the card chrome
byte-equal — radius 8 + the shadow family, ours 358 wide vs the ref's 472).

· the Insights tabs · the Cards view (10 cards) · the row menu (the red Delete
